# Define the list of FAANG ticker symbols for clarity and reusability.
faang_tickers = ('AMZN', 'AAPL', 'GOOGL', 'META', 'NFLX')

# The main query to count the customers.
# It starts by filtering the 'Customers' collection based on two conditions:
# 1. The customer's email address ends with '.com'. This is checked using ENDSWITH and LOWER for case-insensitivity.
# 2. The customer has at least one transaction involving a FAANG stock. This is verified using HAS() on the 'transactions_made' subcollection,
#    which is filtered to include only transactions where the ticker symbol is in the 'faang_tickers' list.
# Finally, COUNT() is used on the filtered customer list to get the total number of distinct customers who meet the criteria.
# The result is calculated at the top-level 'Broker' collection.
faang_customers_count = Broker.CALCULATE(
    num_customers=COUNT(
        Customers.WHERE(
            (ENDSWITH(LOWER(email), '.com')) &
            (HAS(transactions_made.WHERE(ISIN(ticker.symbol, faang_tickers))) == 1)
        )
    )
)

# Define the list of FAANG ticker symbols for clarity
faang_tickers = ('AMZN', 'AAPL', 'GOOGL', 'META', 'NFLX')

# Filter customers based on two conditions:
# 1. Their email ends with '.com' (case-insensitive).
# 2. They have at least one transaction involving a FAANG stock.
faang_customers = Customers.WHERE(
    (ENDSWITH(LOWER(email), '.com')) &
    (HAS(transactions_made.WHERE(ISIN(ticker.symbol, faang_tickers))) == 1)
)

# Count the number of distinct customers that match the criteria.
# Since we started from the Customers collection, each record in 'faang_customers' is unique.
result = GRAPH.CALCULATE(
    distinct_customer_count=COUNT(faang_customers)
)

[DEBUG] [Q4] Code generated by claude:
# Calculate the start of the current week and 8 weeks ago
# We need transactions from 8 weeks ago up to (but not including) the current week
transactions_last_8_weeks = Transactions.WHERE(
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")) >= 1) &  # Exclude current week
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")) <= 8)    # Include last 8 weeks
).CALCULATE(
    week_number = 8 - DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")) + 1,  # Week 1 is 8 weeks ago, Week 8 is last week
    day_of_week = DAYOFWEEK(date_time),
    is_weekend = (DAYNAME(date_time) == "Saturday") | (DAYNAME(date_time) == "Sunday")
)

# Group by week and calculate counts
result = transactions_last_8_weeks.PARTITION(
    name="weeks", 
    by=week_number
).CALCULATE(
    week_number = week_number,
    total_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions = SUM(Transactions.CALCULATE(is_weekend_int = IFF(is_weekend, 1, 0)).is_weekend_int)
).ORDER_BY(week_number.ASC())

# Define the start and end of the 8-week period, excluding the current week.
start_of_period = DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-8 weeks')
end_of_period = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')

# Filter for stock transactions within the specified date range.
recent_stock_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(ticker.ticker_type) == 'stock') &
    (date_time >= start_of_period) &
    (date_time < end_of_period)
)

# For each transaction, determine its week and whether it occurred on a weekend.
transaction_details = recent_stock_transactions.CALCULATE(
    transaction_week = DATETIME(date_time, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYNAME(date_time), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Group transactions by week and count the total and weekend transactions.
weekly_summary = transaction_details.PARTITION(name="weekly_groups", by=transaction_week).CALCULATE(
    week = transaction_week,
    total_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions = SUM(is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week.ASC())

result = weekly_summary

# Calculate the start of the current week and 8 weeks ago
# We need transactions from 8 weeks ago up to (but not including) the current week
transactions_last_8_weeks = Transactions.WHERE(
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")) >= 1) &  # Exclude current week
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")) <= 8)    # Include last 8 weeks
).CALCULATE(
    week_number = 8 - DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")) + 1,  # Week 1 is 8 weeks ago, Week 8 is last week
    day_of_week = DAYOFWEEK(date_time),
    is_weekend = (DAYNAME(date_time) == "Saturday") | (DAYNAME(date_time) == "Sunday")
)

# Group by week and calculate counts
result = transactions_last_8_weeks.PARTITION(
    name="weeks", 
    by=week_number
).CALCULATE(
    week_number = week_number,
    total_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions = SUM(Transactions.CALCULATE(is_weekend_int = IFF(is_weekend, 1, 0)).is_weekend_int)
).ORDER_BY(week_number.ASC())

# Define the start of the current week and the start of the 8-week period.
start_of_current_week = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
start_of_period = DATETIME(start_of_current_week, '-8 weeks')

# Filter transactions to include only stocks within the last 8 weeks (excluding the current week).
stock_transactions_in_period = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(ticker.ticker_type) == 'stock') &
    (date_time >= start_of_period) &
    (date_time < start_of_current_week)
)

# For each transaction, calculate the start of its week and determine if it occurred on a weekend.
transaction_details = stock_transactions_in_period.CALCULATE(
    week_start=DATETIME(date_time, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(date_time), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Group the transactions by the start of the week.
weekly_transaction_groups = transaction_details.PARTITION(name="weeks", by=week_start)

# For each weekly group, count the total transactions and the number of weekend transactions.
result = weekly_transaction_groups.CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=week_start,
    total_transactions=COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions=SUM(Transactions.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week_start_date.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q8] Code generated by claude:
# Get customers who joined in the last 6 months excluding current month
# Calculate the date range: from 6 months ago to the start of current month
recent_customers = Customers.WHERE(
    (join_date >= DATETIME('now', '-6 months', 'start of month')) &
    (join_date < DATETIME('now', 'start of month'))
).CALCULATE(
    join_month = JOIN_STRINGS('-', YEAR(join_date), LPAD(MONTH(join_date), 2, '0'))
)

# Partition by join month to get customer signups per month
monthly_signups = recent_customers.PARTITION(name='months', by=join_month).CALCULATE(
    month = join_month,
    pmcs = COUNT(Customers)
)

# For PMAT, we need to calculate average transaction amount per month
# Get all transactions from these recent customers
customer_transactions = recent_customers.transactions_made.CALCULATE(
    transaction_month = JOIN_STRINGS('-', YEAR(date_time), LPAD(MONTH(date_time), 2, '0')),
    transaction_amount = amount
)

# Partition transactions by month and calculate average
monthly_transactions = customer_transactions.PARTITION(name='tx_months', by=transaction_month).CALCULATE(
    month = transaction_month,
    pmat = AVG(transactions_made.amount)
)

# Combine the results - we need to join PMCS and PMAT by month
# Since we need both metrics for each month, let's use a different approach
# Calculate both metrics in one query by partitioning customers by join month

result = recent_customers.PARTITION(name='signup_months', by=join_month).CALCULATE(
    month = join_month,
    pmcs = COUNT(Customers),
    pmat = AVG(Customers.transactions_made.amount)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# First, filter customers who joined in the last 6 months excluding current month
# We need to calculate the date range
customers_last_6_months = Customers.WHERE(
    (DATEDIFF("months", join_date, DATETIME("now")) <= 6) & 
    (DATEDIFF("months", join_date, DATETIME("now")) >= 1)
).CALCULATE(
    join_month=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(join_date), LPAD(MONTH(join_date), 2, "0")),
    customer_id=_id
)

# Partition by join month to count signups per month
monthly_signups = customers_last_6_months.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=join_month
).CALCULATE(
    month=join_month,
    pmcs=COUNT(Customers)
)

# Now calculate the average transaction amount per month
# We need transactions from these customers grouped by transaction month
transaction_info = customers_last_6_months.transactions_made.CALCULATE(
    tx_month=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date_time), LPAD(MONTH(date_time), 2, "0")),
    tx_amount=amount
)

# Partition transactions by month and calculate average
monthly_transactions = transaction_info.PARTITION(
    name="tx_months",
    by=tx_month
).CALCULATE(
    month=tx_month,
    pmat=AVG(transactions_made.amount)
)

# Join the results - we need both PMCS and PMAT for each month
# Since we need data from two different partitions, let's approach this differently
result = Broker.CALCULATE(
    # Get customers from last 6 months excluding current
    filtered_customers=Customers.WHERE(
        (DATEDIFF("months", join_date, DATETIME("now")) <= 6) & 
        (DATEDIFF("months", join_date, DATETIME("now")) >= 1)
    )
).filtered_customers.CALCULATE(
    join_month=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(join_date), LPAD(MONTH(join_date), 2, "0"))
).PARTITION(
    name="signup_months",
    by=join_month
).CALCULATE(
    month=join_month,
    pmcs=COUNT(filtered_customers),
    pmat=AVG(filtered_customers.transactions_made.amount)
)

# Get customers who signed up in the last 6 months excluding current month
# First, let's calculate the date range
recent_customers = Customers.WHERE(
    (join_date >= DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months')) &
    (join_date < DATETIME('now', 'start of month'))
).CALCULATE(
    signup_month = JOIN_STRINGS('-', YEAR(join_date), LPAD(MONTH(join_date), 2, '0'))
)

# Partition by signup month to calculate PMCS and PMAT
result = recent_customers.PARTITION(name='months', by=signup_month).CALCULATE(
    month = signup_month,
    PMCS = COUNT(Customers),
    PMAT = AVG(Customers.transactions_made.amount)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# First, filter the DailyPrices to include only records from the last 7 days, inclusive of today.
# Then, create a new field 'type_of_ticker' to hold the ticker_type from the related Tickers collection.
recent_daily_prices = DailyPrices.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", date, DATETIME('now')) <= 6
).CALCULATE(
    type_of_ticker=ticker.ticker_type
)

# Partition the filtered data by 'type_of_ticker' to group all records of the same type.
# For each group, calculate the ticker type and the average of the 'close' price.
acp_by_type = recent_daily_prices.PARTITION(
    name="ticker_type_groups", by=type_of_ticker
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_type=type_of_ticker,
    average_closing_price=AVG(DailyPrices.close)
)

# Filter customers who joined in 2022 and have transactions
customers_2022_with_tx = Customers.WHERE(
    (YEAR(join_date) == 2022) & (HAS(transactions_made) == 1)
)

# Partition by country and calculate AR

    country=country,
    AR=100.0 * COUNT(Customers.WHERE(LOWER(status) == LOWER('active'))) / COUNT(Customers)
)

# Step 1: Filter for customers who joined in 2022 and have transactions.
# A new field 'is_active_customer' is created to flag active customers (status is 'active').
customers_info = Customers.WHERE(
    (YEAR(join_date) == 2022) & (HAS(transactions_made) == 1)
).CALCULATE(
    country=country,
    is_active_customer=IFF(LOWER(status) == 'active', 1, 0)
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered customers by country.
# For each country, calculate the Activity Ratio (AR) using the provided formula.
# The number of active customers is the sum of 'is_active_customer', and the total
# is the count of all customers in that group.
activity_ratio_by_country = customers_info.PARTITION(
    name="country_groups", by=country
).CALCULATE(
    country=country,
    AR=(100.0 * SUM(customers_info.is_active_customer) / COUNT(customers_info))
)

# Step 1: Filter transactions to include only 'sell' types from the last month (inclusive).
# Also, extract the ticker symbol to be used as a partition key.
sell_transactions_last_month = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(transaction_type) == 'sell') &
    (date_time >= DATETIME('now', '-1 month'))
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker.symbol
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered transactions by ticker symbol to group them.
ticker_groups = sell_transactions_last_month.PARTITION(name="tickers", by=(ticker_symbol))

# Step 3: For each ticker group, calculate the total sell amount and total costs (tax + commission).
# Then, use a subsequent CALCULATE to compute the SPM using the provided formula.
result = ticker_groups.CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    total_amount_from_sells=SUM(Transactions.amount),
    total_costs=SUM(Transactions.tax) + SUM(Transactions.commission)
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    spm=IFF(
        total_amount_from_sells > 0,
        (total_amount_from_sells - total_costs) / total_amount_from_sells * 100.0,
        0.0
    )
)

# Step 1: Filter transactions to 'sell' types within the last month and extract the ticker symbol for grouping.
sell_transactions_info = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(transaction_type) == 'sell') &
    (date_time >= DATETIME('now', '-1 month'))
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker.symbol
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered transactions by ticker symbol.
# For each ticker group, calculate the sum of amount, tax, and commission.
ticker_groups = sell_transactions_info.PARTITION(
    name="ticker_groups",
    by=(ticker_symbol)
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    total_amount_from_sells=SUM(Transactions.amount),
    total_tax=SUM(Transactions.tax),
    total_commission=SUM(Transactions.commission)
)

# Step 3: Calculate the final SPM using the aggregated values from the previous step.
# This includes a check to prevent division by zero if the total amount is zero.
# The final result includes only the ticker symbol and the calculated SPM.
result = ticker_groups.CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    spm=IFF(
        total_amount_from_sells > 0,
        (total_amount_from_sells - (total_tax + total_commission)) / total_amount_from_sells * 100.0,
        0.0
    )
)

# Step 1: Extract month and symbol from daily prices
price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    symbol = ticker.symbol,
    month = JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0")),
    close = close,
    high = high,
    low = low
)

# Step 2: Partition by symbol and month to calculate monthly statistics
monthly_stats = price_info.PARTITION(
    name="symbol_month", 
    by=(symbol, month)
).CALCULATE(
    symbol = symbol,
    month = month,
    avg_close = AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high = MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low = MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Calculate MoMC using PREV to get previous month's average
result = monthly_stats.CALCULATE(
    symbol = symbol,
    month = month,
    avg_close = avg_close,
    max_high = max_high,
    min_low = min_low,
    prev_avg_close = PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC()), per="Broker", default=0),
    momc = IFF(
        PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC()), per="Broker", default=0) == 0,
        0,
        (avg_close - PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC()), per="Broker", default=0)) / PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC()), per="Broker", default=0)
    )
).CALCULATE(
    symbol,
    month,
    avg_close,
    max_high,
    min_low,
    momc
).ORDER_BY(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC())

)

    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,

    max_high=MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low=MIN(DailyPrices.low)

[DEBUG] [Q13] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Create a base collection with the necessary calculated fields: ticker symbol and a formatted month string for grouping and sorting.
price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    symbol=ticker.symbol,
    month_str=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0"))
)

# Step 2: Partition the daily prices by symbol and month to calculate the monthly aggregates.
# This creates a collection where each record contains the stats for a specific ticker in a specific month.
monthly_stats = price_info.PARTITION(name="monthly_groups", by=(symbol, month_str)).CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month_str,
    avg_close=AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high=MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low=MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Partition the monthly stats by symbol. This allows us to use the PREV window function
# to get the previous month's data for each ticker separately.
# The 'per="ticker_groups"' argument in PREV ensures the calculation is contained within each ticker's data.
result = monthly_stats.PARTITION(name="ticker_groups", by=(symbol)).monthly_groups.CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    average_closing_price=avg_close,
    highest_price=max_high,
    lowest_price=min_low,
    prev_avg_close=PREV(avg_close, by=month.ASC(), per="ticker_groups")
).CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    average_closing_price=average_closing_price,
    highest_price=highest_price,
    lowest_price=lowest_price,
    # Calculate MoMC, handling cases where the previous month's average is null or zero to avoid errors.
    MoMC=IFF(
        (prev_avg_close == None) | (prev_avg_close == 0),
        None,
        (average_closing_price - prev_avg_close) / prev_avg_close
    )
)

# Step 1: Prepare daily price data with symbol and a sortable month string (YYYY-MM)
price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    symbol=ticker.symbol,
    month_str=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0"))
)

# Step 2: Partition by ticker and month to calculate monthly aggregates
monthly_stats = price_info.PARTITION(name="ticker_months", by=(symbol, month_str)).CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month_str,
    avg_close=AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high=MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low=MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Partition the aggregated monthly stats by symbol to facilitate the MoM calculation
# This creates groups where each group contains all monthly data for a single ticker
ticker_groups = monthly_stats.PARTITION(name="tickers", by=symbol)

# Step 4: Within each ticker's group of months, calculate the MoMC
# Use the PREV window function to get the prior month's average closing price
result = ticker_groups.monthly_stats.CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    avg_close=avg_close,
    max_high=max_high,
    min_low=min_low,
    # Get the previous month's average close, ordered by month, for each ticker
    prev_avg_close=PREV(avg_close, by=month.ASC(), per="tickers")
).CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    avg_close=avg_close,
    max_high=max_high,
    min_low=min_low,
    # Calculate MoMC, handling cases where the previous month's data is null or zero
    momc=IFF(
        (prev_avg_close == None) | (prev_avg_close == 0),
        None,
        (avg_close - prev_avg_close) / prev_avg_close
    )
)

# Filter daily prices for the date range and calculate price metrics per ticker
price_data = DailyPrices.WHERE(
    (date >= "2023-04-01") & (date <= "2023-04-04")
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol = ticker.symbol,
    high_price = high,
    low_price = low
)

# Partition by ticker symbol to calculate max high and min low for each ticker
ticker_price_changes = price_data.PARTITION(
    name="tickers", 
    by=ticker_symbol
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol = ticker_symbol,
    price_change = MAX(DailyPrices.high_price) - MIN(DailyPrices.low_price)
)

# Get the top 3 tickers by price change
result = ticker_price_changes.TOP_K(3, by=price_change.DESC())

price_data = DailyPrices.WHERE(
    (date >= "2023-04-01") & (date <= "2023-04-04")
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol = ticker.symbol,
    high_price = high,
    low_price = low
)

# Partition by ticker symbol to calculate max high and min low for each ticker
ticker_price_changes = price_data.PARTITION(
    name="tickers", 
    by=ticker_symbol
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol = ticker_symbol,
    price_change = MAX(DailyPrices.high_price) - MIN(DailyPrices.low_price)
)

result = ticker_price_changes.TOP_K(3, by=price_change.DESC())

# Filter the daily prices to the specified date range from April 1, 2023, to April 4, 2023.
# Also, calculate the ticker_symbol to be used for partitioning.
filtered_daily_prices = DailyPrices.WHERE(
    (date >= '2023-04-01') & (date <= '2023-04-04')
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker.symbol
)

# Partition the filtered data by ticker symbol to group all price records for each stock.
stocks_by_ticker = filtered_daily_prices.PARTITION(
    name="stocks", by=(ticker_symbol)
)

# For each stock group, calculate the price change by subtracting the minimum low from the maximum high
# over the period. Then, select the top 3 stocks with the largest price change.
result = stocks_by_ticker.CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    price_change=MAX(DailyPrices.high) - MIN(DailyPrices.low)
).TOP_K(3, by=price_change.DESC())

# Calculate join year/month and count transactions in that same month
customer_signup_transactions = Customers.CALCULATE(
    customer_id=_id,
    customer_name=name,
    join_year=YEAR(join_date),
    join_month=MONTH(join_date),
    num_transactions_in_join_month=COUNT(
        transactions_made.WHERE(
            (YEAR(date_time) == join_year) & 
            (MONTH(date_time) == join_month)
        )
    )
)

).CALCULATE(

    (date_time >= "2023-01-01") & (date_time <= "2023-03-31")
)

[DEBUG] [Q17] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Filter transactions to the specified date range from Jan 1, 2023, to Mar 31, 2023.
# Using '< "2023-04-01"' is a robust way to include all timestamps on March 31st.
transactions_in_range = Transactions.WHERE(
    (date_time >= "2023-01-01") & (date_time < "2023-04-01")
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered transactions by their type.
# For each type, calculate the number of distinct customers and the average number of shares.
transaction_type_stats = transactions_in_range.PARTITION(
    name="groups_by_type", by=transaction_type
).CALCULATE(
    transaction_type=transaction_type,
    num_distinct_customers=NDISTINCT(Transactions.customer_id),
    avg_shares=AVG(Transactions.shares)
)

# Step 3: Select the top 3 transaction types based on the number of distinct customers, sorted in descending order.
top_transaction_types = transaction_type_stats.TOP_K(
    3, by=num_distinct_customers.DESC()
)

# First, prepare transaction data with customer state and ticker type
transaction_info = Transactions.CALCULATE(
    customer_state = customer.state,
    ticker_type = ticker.ticker_type
)

# Partition by the combination of customer state and ticker type
state_type_groups = transaction_info.PARTITION(
    name="state_type_combos", 
    by=(customer_state, ticker_type)
).CALCULATE(
    customer_state = customer_state,
    ticker_type = ticker_type,
    num_transactions = COUNT(Transactions)
)

# Get the top 5 combinations
result = state_type_groups.TOP_K(5, by=num_transactions.DESC())

transaction_info = Transactions.CALCULATE(
    customer_state = customer.state,
    ticker_type = ticker.ticker_type
)

# Partition by the combination of customer state and ticker type
state_type_combinations = transaction_info.PARTITION(
    name="state_type_groups", 
    by=(customer_state, ticker_type)
).CALCULATE(
    customer_state = customer_state,
    ticker_type = ticker_type,
    num_transactions = COUNT(Transactions)
)

[DEBUG] [Q24] Code generated by gemini:
# First, we access the Transactions collection and calculate the customer's state and the ticker's type for each transaction.
# These values are needed for partitioning.
transaction_info = Transactions.CALCULATE(
    customer_state=customer.state,
    ticker_type_value=ticker.ticker_type
)

# Next, we partition the transactions by the combination of customer state and ticker type.
# For each group, we calculate the state, ticker type, and the total number of transactions.
top_combinations = transaction_info.PARTITION(
    name="state_type_combinations", by=(customer_state, ticker_type_value)
).CALCULATE(
    customer_state=customer_state,
    ticker_type=ticker_type_value,
    number_of_transactions=COUNT(Transactions)
)

# Finally, we select the top 5 combinations based on the number of transactions in descending order.
result = top_combinations.TOP_K(5, by=number_of_transactions.DESC())

[DEBUG] [Q24] Code generated by gemini:
# First, we access the Transactions collection and calculate the customer's state and the ticker's type for each transaction.
# These fields are needed for grouping and must be calculated before partitioning.
transaction_info = Transactions.CALCULATE(
    customer_state=customer.state,
    ticker_type=ticker.ticker_type
)

# Next, we partition the transaction_info collection by the combination of customer_state and ticker_type.
# For each unique combination, we count the number of transactions.
combinations = transaction_info.PARTITION(
    name="state_ticker_groups",
    by=(customer_state, ticker_type)
).CALCULATE(
    customer_state=customer_state,
    ticker_type=ticker_type,
    num_transactions=COUNT(Transactions)
)

# Finally, we select the top 5 combinations with the highest number of transactions,
# ordering the results in descending order.
result = combinations.TOP_K(5, by=num_transactions.DESC())

# Filter transactions from the past 30 days (inclusive)
recent_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", date_time, DATETIME("now")) <= 30
)

# Add country information to each transaction
transactions_with_country = recent_transactions.CALCULATE(
    country = customer.country,
    amount = amount
)

# Group by country and calculate aggregates
country_stats = transactions_with_country.PARTITION(
    name="countries", 
    by=country
).CALCULATE(
    country = country,
    num_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    total_amount = SUM(Transactions.amount)
)

# Get top 5 countries by total amount
result = country_stats.TOP_K(5, by=total_amount.DESC())

# Filter transactions from the past 30 days (inclusive)
recent_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", date_time, DATETIME("now")) <= 30
)

# Add country information to each transaction
transactions_with_country = recent_transactions.CALCULATE(
    country = customer.country,
    amount = amount
)

# Group by country and calculate metrics
country_stats = transactions_with_country.PARTITION(
    name="countries", 
    by=country
).CALCULATE(
    country = country,
    num_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    total_amount = SUM(Transactions.amount)
)

# Get top 5 countries by total amount
result = country_stats.TOP_K(5, by=total_amount.DESC())

# Filter for successful transactions in Q1 2023 and extract year/month
q1_2023_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(status) == 'success') & 
    (YEAR(date_time) == 2023) & 
    (MONTH(date_time) >= 1) & 
    (MONTH(date_time) <= 3)
).CALCULATE(
    year = YEAR(date_time),
    month = MONTH(date_time),
    price = price
)

# Partition by year and month, then calculate average price
result = q1_2023_transactions.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups", 
    by=(year, month)
).CALCULATE(
    year = year,
    month = month,
    avg_price = AVG(Transactions.price)
).ORDER_BY(year.ASC(), month.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q32] Code generated by claude:
# Get high-value sales and their payments
high_value_payments = Sales.WHERE(sale_price > 30000).payment.CALCULATE(
    payment_date=payment_date,
    week_start=DATETIME(payment_date, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(payment_date), (1, 7))  # Sunday=1, Saturday=7
)

# Filter to last 8 weeks excluding current week
current_week_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
eight_weeks_ago = DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-56 days')  # 8 weeks * 7 days

filtered_payments = high_value_payments.WHERE(
    (week_start >= eight_weeks_ago) & (week_start < current_week_start)
)

# Group by week and count payments
result = filtered_payments.PARTITION(name="weeks", by=week_start).CALCULATE(
    week=week_start,
    total_payments_received=COUNT(payment),
    weekend_payments_received=SUM(payment.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week.ASC())

weekly_payment_summary = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    # Define the time window: from the start of 8 weeks ago up to (but not including) the start of the current week.
    start_of_8_weeks_ago=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-8 weeks'),
    start_of_current_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
).Sales.WHERE(
    # 1. Filter for sales with a price over $30,000.
    sale_price > 30000
).payment.WHERE(
    # 2. Filter payments to be within the last 8 weeks (excluding the current week).
    (payment_date >= start_of_8_weeks_ago) & (payment_date < start_of_current_week)
).CALCULATE(
    # 3. For each payment, determine the start of its week and if it occurred on a weekend.
    week_start_date=DATETIME(payment_date, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(payment_date), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
).PARTITION(
    # 4. Group the filtered payments by the start of the week.
    name="weekly_groups", by=(week_start_date)
).CALCULATE(
    # 5. For each weekly group, calculate the required metrics.
    week=week_start_date,
    total_payments_received=COUNT(payment),
    weekend_payments_received=SUM(payment.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(
    # 6. Sort the final results by week in ascending order.
    week.ASC()
)

# Get high-value sales and their payments with week information
high_value_payments = Sales.WHERE(sale_price > 30000).payment.CALCULATE(
    payment_date=payment_date,
    week_start=DATETIME(payment_date, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(payment_date), (1, 7))  # Sunday=1, Saturday=7
)

# Calculate current week start to filter out current week
current_week_info = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    current_week_start=DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
)

# Filter payments from the last 8 weeks (excluding current week)
recent_payments = current_week_info.high_value_payments.WHERE(
    (week_start < current_week_start) & 
    (DATEDIFF('weeks', week_start, current_week_start) <= 8)
)

# Group by week and count payments
result = recent_payments.PARTITION(name='weeks', by=week_start).CALCULATE(
    week=week_start,
    total_payments_received=COUNT(payment),
    weekend_payments_received=COUNT(payment.WHERE(is_weekend))
).ORDER_BY(week.ASC())

# Define the time window boundaries once for efficiency.
time_boundaries = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    start_of_current_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week'),
    start_of_8_weeks_ago=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-8 weeks')
)

# Filter payments from high-value sales that fall within the last 8 full weeks.
# Also, calculate helper fields for grouping and aggregation.
payments_to_analyze = time_boundaries.PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    (sale_record.sale_price > 30000) &
    (payment_date >= start_of_8_weeks_ago) &
    (payment_date < start_of_current_week)
).CALCULATE(
    week_start=DATETIME(payment_date, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(payment_date), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Partition the payments by week and calculate the total and weekend payment counts.
weekly_payment_summary = payments_to_analyze.PARTITION(name="weeks", by=(week_start)).CALCULATE(
    week=week_start,
    total_payments_received=COUNT(PaymentsReceived),
    weekend_payments_received=SUM(PaymentsReceived.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week.ASC())

# First, filter the 'Sales' collection to isolate sales of Toyota cars that occurred within the last 30 days.
# A contextless expression is used to store this filtered set for clarity and reuse.
toyota_sales_last_30_days = Sales.WHERE(
    (LOWER(car.make) == 'toyota') & (DATEDIFF("days", sale_date, DATETIME('now')) < 30)
)

# Then, use the top-level 'Dealership' collection to perform aggregate calculations on the filtered sales data.
# COUNT is used to get the total number of sales, and SUM is used to calculate the total revenue from the 'sale_price' of those sales.
result = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    number_of_sales=COUNT(toyota_sales_last_30_days),
    total_revenue=SUM(toyota_sales_last_30_days.sale_price)
)

).CALCULATE(

    car_model = model,

        RANKING(by=snapshot_date.DESC(), per="Cars", allow_ties=False) == 1
    ).SINGULAR().is_in_inventory,
    # Get the maximum sale price for this car

)

    current_inventory_status == False

# Define a contextless expression to efficiently get the latest inventory snapshot for each car.
# This is done by ranking the snapshots by date in descending order for each car and picking the top one.
# SINGULAR() is used to treat the result as a single record, allowing direct field access.
latest_inventory_status = Cars.inventory_snapshots.WHERE(
    RANKING(by=snapshot_date.DESC(), per="Cars") == 1
).SINGULAR()

# Start with the Cars collection and filter for cars that meet two criteria:
# 1. The car has at least one sale record (HAS(sale_records) == 1).
# 2. The car's most recent inventory status is 'not in inventory' (is_in_inventory == False).
sold_and_out_of_stock_cars = Cars.WHERE(
    (HAS(sale_records) == 1) & (latest_inventory_status.is_in_inventory == False)
)

# Partition the filtered cars by make and model to group them.
# For each group, calculate the make, model, and the maximum sale price from all associated sale records.
# Finally, order the results by the highest sale price in descending order.
highest_sales_by_make_model = sold_and_out_of_stock_cars.PARTITION(
    name="car_groups", by=(make, model)
).CALCULATE(
    make=make,
    model=model,
    highest_sale_price=MAX(Cars.sale_records.sale_price)
).ORDER_BY(highest_sale_price.DESC())

# Get all sales from salespersons hired between 2022-2023 in the last 6 months
qualified_sales = Salespersons.WHERE(
    (YEAR(hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(hire_date) <= 2023)
).sales_made.WHERE(
    (sale_date >= DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months')) &
    (sale_date < DATETIME('now', 'start of month'))
).CALCULATE(
    sale_month=DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month'),
    sale_price=sale_price
)

# Partition by month to get monthly metrics
result = qualified_sales.PARTITION(
    name="months",
    by=sale_month
).CALCULATE(
    month=sale_month,
    pmsps=COUNT(Sales),  # Per month salesperson sales count
    pmsr=SUM(Sales.sale_price)  # Per month sales revenue in dollars
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q39] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the date range at the top level for access throughout the query.
# This establishes the start (6 months ago) and end (current month) of our window.
date_range_def = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    start_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months'),
    end_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
)

# Start with all sales and calculate the month of each sale. This creates a "scaffold"
# of all months that have at least one sale, ensuring we can report on months with
# zero sales for our specific salesperson group.
sales_with_month = date_range_def.Sales.CALCULATE(
    sale_month = DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month')
)

# Partition all sales by the calculated month. This groups all sales into monthly buckets.
monthly_groups = sales_with_month.PARTITION(name="monthly_sales", by=(sale_month))

# For each month group, calculate the required metrics.
# The filtering for salespersons (hired between 2022-2023) is applied *inside*
# the aggregation functions. This is the key to getting 0s for months that have
# sales, but not from the target salesperson group.
monthly_metrics = monthly_groups.CALCULATE(
    month = sale_month,
    # PMSPS: Count sales where the salesperson was hired in the target years.
    PMSPS = COUNT(Sales.WHERE(
        (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) <= 2023)
    )),
    # PMSR: Sum sale_price for the same filtered group. Default to 0.0 if no such sales exist.
    PMSR = DEFAULT_TO(SUM(Sales.WHERE(
        (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) <= 2023)
    ).sale_price), 0.0)
)

# Finally, filter the aggregated monthly results to our desired time window (last 6 months)
# and order them chronologically.
pmsps_and_pmsr_metrics = monthly_metrics.WHERE(
    (month >= start_date) & (month < end_date)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q39] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the date range for the last 6 months, excluding the current month.
start_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months')
end_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

# First, create a "scaffold" of all months in the last 6 months that have at least one sale.
# This is done by taking all sales, calculating their month, filtering for the date range,
# and then partitioning by month. This approach ensures that months with zero sales
# from the target group but non-zero sales from other groups are included.
sales_with_month = Sales.CALCULATE(
    sale_month=DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month')
)

recent_months_scaffold = sales_with_month.WHERE(
    (sale_month >= start_date) & (sale_month < end_date)
).PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups",
    by=(sale_month)
)

# Now, for each month in our scaffold, calculate the required metrics.
# The metrics are calculated by filtering the 'Sales' sub-collection within each partition
# to only include sales from salespersons hired between 2022 and 2023.
sales_metrics = recent_months_scaffold.CALCULATE(
    month=sale_month,
    # PMSPS: Count sales from the target salespersons for the given month.
    PMSPS=COUNT(
        Sales.WHERE(
            (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) <= 2023)
        )
    ),
    # PMSR: Sum the sale_price for the same filtered set of sales.
    # Use DEFAULT_TO to ensure months with no relevant sales show 0.0 instead of NULL.
    PMSR=DEFAULT_TO(
        SUM(
            Sales.WHERE(
                (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) <= 2023)
            ).sale_price
        ),
        0.0
    )
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# The final result is assigned to the variable 'result'.
result = sales_metrics

# Get the date range for the last 6 months excluding current month
current_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
six_months_ago = DATETIME(current_month_start, '-6 months')

# Filter salespersons hired between 2022 and 2023 and get their sales
eligible_sales = Salespersons.WHERE(
    (YEAR(hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(hire_date) <= 2023)
).sales_made.WHERE(
    (sale_date >= six_months_ago) & (sale_date < current_month_start)
).CALCULATE(
    salesperson_id=salesperson.salesperson_id,
    first_name=salesperson.first_name,
    last_name=salesperson.last_name,
    sale_month=DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month'),
    sale_price=sale_price
)

# Partition by salesperson and month
result = eligible_sales.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups",
    by=(salesperson_id, first_name, last_name, sale_month)
).CALCULATE(
    salesperson_id=salesperson_id,
    first_name=first_name,
    last_name=last_name,
    month=sale_month,
    pmsps=COUNT(sales_made),  # Per month salesperson sales count
    pmsr=SUM(sales_made.sale_price)  # Per month sales revenue
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Define the date boundaries for the last 6 months, excluding the current month.
date_boundaries = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    start_date=DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months'),
    end_date=DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
)

# Filter sales to the specified date range and for salespersons hired in 2022-2023.
# Also, create a 'sale_month' column for grouping.
sales_to_group = date_boundaries.Sales.WHERE(
    (sale_date >= start_date) &
    (sale_date < end_date) &
    (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) >= 2022) &
    (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) <= 2023)
).CALCULATE(
    sale_month=DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month')
)

# Partition the filtered sales by month to calculate the required metrics.
# The result is ordered by month in ascending order.
monthly_summary = sales_to_group.PARTITION(
    name="sales_by_month",
    by=(sale_month)
).CALCULATE(
    month=sale_month,
    PMSPS=COUNT(Sales),
    PMSR=SUM(Sales.sale_price)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# First, for each payment received, calculate the number of days that passed since the sale date.
# This implicitly handles the condition "Only take sales with payments" because we start from the PaymentsReceived collection.
payment_durations = PaymentsReceived.CALCULATE(
    days_difference=DATEDIFF("days", sale_record.sale_date, payment_date)
)

# Next, calculate the average of all these durations and round the result to 2 decimal places.
average_payment_time = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    avg_days_to_pay=ROUND(AVG(payment_durations.days_difference), 2)
)

[DEBUG] [Q43] Code generated by claude:
# Find cars that went out of inventory and were sold on the same day
cars_sold_same_day = Cars.WHERE(
    HAS(inventory_snapshots.WHERE(is_in_inventory == False)) == 1
).CALCULATE(
    car_make = make,
    car_model = model,
    # Get the date when the car went out of inventory (is_in_inventory = False)
    out_of_inventory_date = inventory_snapshots.WHERE(
        is_in_inventory == False
    ).SINGULAR().snapshot_date,
    # Get the sale information
    sale_price = sale_records.SINGULAR().sale_price,
    sale_date = sale_records.SINGULAR().sale_date
).WHERE(
    # Filter for cars where sale date matches the out of inventory date
    sale_date == out_of_inventory_date
)

cars_sold_same_day = Cars.WHERE(
    HAS(inventory_snapshots.WHERE(is_in_inventory == False)) == 1
).CALCULATE(
    car_make = make,
    car_model = model,
    # Get the date when the car went out of inventory (is_in_inventory = False)
    out_of_inventory_date = inventory_snapshots.WHERE(
        is_in_inventory == False
    ).SINGULAR().snapshot_date,
    # Get the sale information
    sale_price = sale_records.SINGULAR().sale_price,
    sale_date = sale_records.SINGULAR().sale_date
).WHERE(

)

# Step 1: Extract year and month from payment dates and calculate monthly totals
payments_with_month = PaymentsReceived.CALCULATE(
    payment_year = YEAR(payment_date),
    payment_month = MONTH(payment_date)
)

# Step 2: Partition by year and month to get monthly totals
monthly_totals = payments_with_month.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=(payment_year, payment_month)
).CALCULATE(
    year = payment_year,
    month = payment_month,
    total_payments = SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
)

# Step 3: Calculate MoM change using PREV window function
result = monthly_totals.CALCULATE(
    year = year,
    month = month,
    total_payments = total_payments,
    prev_month_payments = PREV(
        total_payments, 
        by=(year.ASC(), month.ASC()),
        default=0
    ),
    mom_change = total_payments - PREV(
        total_payments, 
        by=(year.ASC(), month.ASC()),
        default=0
    )
).ORDER_BY(year.ASC(), month.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q44] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Extract year and month from each payment date.
payments_by_month = Dealership.PaymentsReceived.CALCULATE(
    payment_year=YEAR(payment_date),
    payment_month=MONTH(payment_date)
)

# Step 2: Group payments by year and month, and calculate the total payment amount for each group.
# The result is ordered by year and month to prepare for the MoM calculation.
monthly_totals = payments_by_month.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups",
    by=(payment_year, payment_month)
).CALCULATE(
    payment_year=payment_year,
    payment_month=payment_month,
    total_payments=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
).ORDER_BY(payment_year.ASC(), payment_month.ASC())

# Step 3: Calculate the Month-over-Month (MoM) change using the PREV window function.
# PREV retrieves the total_payments from the previous month based on the specified order.
result = monthly_totals.CALCULATE(
    payment_year=payment_year,
    payment_month=payment_month,
    total_payments=total_payments,
    mom_change=total_payments - PREV(total_payments, by=(payment_year.ASC(), payment_month.ASC()))
)

# Get payments from the previous ISO week
previous_week_payments = PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    (payment_date >= DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-7 days')) &
    (payment_date < DATETIME('now', 'start of week'))
)

# Partition by date and payment method, then calculate daily totals
result = previous_week_payments.PARTITION(
    name="daily_method_groups", 
    by=(payment_date, payment_method)
).CALCULATE(
    payment_date=payment_date,
    payment_method=payment_method,
    daily_total=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
).ORDER_BY(payment_date.ASC(), payment_method.ASC())

# Define the start of the current week and the start of the previous week.
# An ISO week starts on Monday.
start_of_current_week = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
start_of_previous_week = DATETIME(start_of_current_week, '-7 days')

# Filter payments to only include those from the previous full week.
payments_in_previous_week = Dealership.PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    (payment_date >= start_of_previous_week) & (payment_date < start_of_current_week)
)

# Partition the filtered payments by date and method, then sum the amounts for each group.
daily_payment_totals = payments_in_previous_week.PARTITION(
    name="daily_method_groups",
    by=(payment_date, payment_method)
).CALCULATE(
    payment_date=payment_date,
    payment_method=payment_method,
    total_amount=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
)

)

    (payment_date >= start_of_previous_week) & (payment_date <= end_of_previous_week)

[DEBUG] [Q61] Code generated by claude:
# First, prepare sales data with year, quarter, and customer state
sales_2023 = Sales.WHERE(YEAR(sale_date) == 2023).CALCULATE(
    sale_year = YEAR(sale_date),
    sale_quarter = IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 3, 1,
                      IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 6, 2,
                          IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 9, 3, 4))),
    customer_state = customer.state,
    sale_price = sale_price
)

# Partition by state and quarter to calculate totals
quarterly_sales = sales_2023.PARTITION(
    name="state_quarter_groups", 
    by=(customer_state, sale_quarter)
).CALCULATE(
    state = customer_state,
    quarter = sale_quarter,
    quarter_start_date = IFF(sale_quarter == 1, "2023-01-01",
                            IFF(sale_quarter == 2, "2023-04-01",
                                IFF(sale_quarter == 3, "2023-07-01", "2023-10-01"))),
    total_sales = SUM(Sales.sale_price)
).ORDER_BY(state.ASC(), quarter.ASC())

result = quarterly_sales

# First, filter sales to only include those from the year 2023.
sales_in_2023 = Sales.WHERE(YEAR(sale_date) == 2023)

# For each sale, calculate the customer's state and the first date of the quarter.
# A nested IFF statement is used to map the month to the correct quarter start date.
sales_with_quarter_info = sales_in_2023.CALCULATE(
    customer_state=customer.state,
    quarter_date=IFF(
        MONTH(sale_date) <= 3,
        DATETIME('2023-01-01'),
        IFF(
            MONTH(sale_date) <= 6,
            DATETIME('2023-04-01'),
            IFF(
                MONTH(sale_date) <= 9,
                DATETIME('2023-07-01'),
                DATETIME('2023-10-01')
            )
        )
    )
)

# Partition the data by state and quarter, then calculate the total sales for each group.
quarterly_sales_by_state = sales_with_quarter_info.PARTITION(
    name="state_quarter_groups",
    by=(customer_state, quarter_date)
).CALCULATE(
    state=customer_state,
    quarter=quarter_date,
    total_quarterly_sales=SUM(Sales.sale_price)
)

[DEBUG] [Q61] Code generated by gemini:
# First, filter sales to include only those from the year 2023.
sales_in_2023 = Sales.WHERE(YEAR(sale_date) == 2023)

# Next, calculate the necessary fields for partitioning: the customer's state and the start date of the quarter for each sale.
# The quarter's start date is determined using nested IFF functions based on the month of the sale.
sales_with_quarter_info = sales_in_2023.CALCULATE(
    customer_state=customer.state,
    quarter_start_date=IFF(
        ISIN(MONTH(sale_date), (1, 2, 3)),
        DATETIME('2023-01-01'),
        IFF(
            ISIN(MONTH(sale_date), (4, 5, 6)),
            DATETIME('2023-04-01'),
            IFF(
                ISIN(MONTH(sale_date), (7, 8, 9)),
                DATETIME('2023-07-01'),
                DATETIME('2023-10-01')
            )
        )
    )
)

# Partition the sales data by state and the calculated quarter start date.
# Then, for each group, calculate the total sales by summing the sale_price.
quarterly_sales_by_state = sales_with_quarter_info.PARTITION(
    name="state_quarter_groups",
    by=(customer_state, quarter_start_date)
).CALCULATE(
    state=customer_state,
    quarter=quarter_start_date,
    total_sales=SUM(Sales.sale_price)
)

# First, filter sales to include only those from the year 2023.
sales_in_2023 = Sales.WHERE(YEAR(sale_date) == 2023)

# Next, calculate the necessary fields for partitioning: the customer's state and the start date of the quarter for each sale.
# The quarter's start date is determined using nested IFF functions based on the month of the sale.
sales_with_quarter_info = sales_in_2023.CALCULATE(
    customer_state=customer.state,
    quarter_start_date=IFF(
        ISIN(MONTH(sale_date), (1, 2, 3)),
        DATETIME('2023-01-01'),
        IFF(
            ISIN(MONTH(sale_date), (4, 5, 6)),
            DATETIME('2023-04-01'),
            IFF(
                ISIN(MONTH(sale_date), (7, 8, 9)),
                DATETIME('2023-07-01'),
                DATETIME('2023-10-01')
            )
        )
    )
)

# Partition the sales data by state and the calculated quarter start date.
# Then, for each group, calculate the total sales by summing the sale_price.
quarterly_sales_by_state = sales_with_quarter_info.PARTITION(
    name="state_quarter_groups",
    by=(customer_state, quarter_start_date)
).CALCULATE(
    state=customer_state,
    quarter=quarter_start_date,
    total_sales=SUM(Sales.sale_price)
)

    drug_name=drug_name,
    average_ddd=AVG(
        treatments_used_in.WHERE(

        ).CALCULATE(

        ).WHERE(
            treatment_duration > 0
        ).CALCULATE(
            ddd=tot_drug_amt / treatment_duration
        ).ddd
    )

# First, let's get treatments from the last 3 months (excluding current month)
# We'll calculate the month for each treatment and filter accordingly
treatments_with_month = Treatments.CALCULATE(
    treatment_month = JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(start_dt), LPAD(MONTH(start_dt), 2, "0")),
    patient_id = patient_id,
    is_biologic = LOWER(drug.drug_type) == LOWER('biologic')
).WHERE(
    # Filter for last 3 months excluding current month
    (DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME('now')) >= 1) & 
    (DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME('now')) <= 3)
)

# Now partition by month to count distinct patients
monthly_stats = treatments_with_month.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=treatment_month
).CALCULATE(
    month = treatment_month,
    patient_count = NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient_id),
    biologic_treatment_count = NDISTINCT(Treatments.WHERE(is_biologic == True).patient_id)
)

# Order by month descending to show most recent first
result = monthly_stats.ORDER_BY(month.DESC())

# Define the date range for the last 3 full months

    start_of_current_month=DATETIME('now', 'start of month'),
    start_of_3_months_ago=DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-3 months')
)

# Filter treatments within the date range and prepare data for aggregation
recent_treatments = date_info.Treatments.WHERE(
    (start_dt >= start_of_3_months_ago) & (start_dt < start_of_current_month)
).CALCULATE(
    treatment_month=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(start_dt), MONTH(start_dt)),
    patient_id=patient_id,
    is_biologic=(LOWER(drug.drug_type) == 'biologic')
)

# Partition by month and calculate the distinct patient counts
monthly_patient_counts = recent_treatments.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups", by=(treatment_month,)
).CALCULATE(
    month=treatment_month,
    patient_count=NDISTINCT(recent_treatments.patient_id),
    biologic_treatment_count=NDISTINCT(recent_treatments.WHERE(is_biologic).patient_id)

# 1. Define the time window at the graph level for the last 3 full months
date_info = DermTreatment.CALCULATE(
    start_of_period=DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-3 months'),
    end_of_period=DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
)

# 2. Filter treatments within this period and calculate necessary fields for partitioning
treatments_info = date_info.Treatments.WHERE(
    (start_dt >= start_of_period) & (start_dt < end_of_period)
).CALCULATE(
    treatment_month=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(start_dt), LPAD(MONTH(start_dt), 2, "0")),
    is_biologic_treatment=(LOWER(drug.drug_type) == 'biologic'),
    patient_id=patient_id
)

# 3. Partition by month and calculate the distinct counts
monthly_patient_counts = treatments_info.PARTITION(name="monthly_groups", by=(treatment_month)).CALCULATE(
    month=treatment_month,
    patient_count=NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient_id),
    biologic_treatment_count=NDISTINCT(KEEP_IF(Treatments.patient_id, Treatments.is_biologic_treatment))
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Step 1: Calculate the number of distinct drugs prescribed by each doctor.
# We also select the 'speciality' field, which will be used for partitioning.
doctor_info = DermTreatment.Doctors.CALCULATE(
    speciality=speciality,
    num_distinct_drugs=NDISTINCT(prescribed_treatments.drug_id)
)

# Step 2: Partition the doctors by their specialty to create groups.
specialty_groups = doctor_info.PARTITION(name="specialties", by=(speciality))

# Step 3: Access the doctors within each specialty group and calculate their rank.
# The RANKING function is used with the 'per' argument to rank doctors within their respective "specialties" group.
# The rank is based on the number of distinct drugs prescribed, in descending order.
result = specialty_groups.doctor_info.CALCULATE(
    doc_id=doc_id,
    speciality=speciality,
    num_distinct_drugs=num_distinct_drugs,
    SDRSDR=RANKING(by=num_distinct_drugs.DESC(), per="specialties")
)

# Get treatments from the last 12 months, excluding the current month
# Calculate the start of the current month and go back 12 months
treatments_with_month = Treatments.WHERE(
    (start_dt >= DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-12 months')) &
    (start_dt < DATETIME('now', 'start of month'))
).CALCULATE(
    treatment_month=DATETIME(start_dt, 'start of month'),
    patient_id=patient_id,
    diag_id=diag_id
)

# Partition by month and calculate PMPD and PMTC
result = treatments_with_month.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=treatment_month
).CALCULATE(
    month=treatment_month,
    PMPD=NDISTINCT(Treatments.CALCULATE(patient_diag=JOIN_STRINGS('-', patient_id, diag_id)).patient_diag),
    PMTC=COUNT(Treatments)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q71] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the time window for the last 12 full months
time_boundaries = DermTreatment.CALCULATE(
    start_of_current_month=DATETIME('now', 'start of month'),
    start_of_period=DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-12 months')
)

# Filter treatments to the specified period and create a 'treatment_month' field for aggregation
treatments_in_period = time_boundaries.Treatments.WHERE(
    (start_dt >= start_of_period) & (start_dt < start_of_current_month)
).CALCULATE(
    treatment_month=DATETIME(start_dt, 'start of month')
)

# Partition the filtered treatments by month and calculate PMPD and PMTC for each month
monthly_metrics = treatments_in_period.PARTITION(name="monthly_groups", by=(treatment_month)).CALCULATE(
    month=treatment_month,
    PMPD=NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient_id),
    PMTC=COUNT(Treatments)
).ORDER_BY(treatment_month.ASC())

)

# Step 1: For each patient, identify their first treatment and extract the year.
# A "new patient" is defined by the year of their first treatment.
# We use RANKING per patient on their treatments, ordered by start date.
first_treatment_years = Patients.treatments_received.WHERE(
    RANKING(by=start_dt.ASC(), per="Patients") == 1
).CALCULATE(
    new_patient_year=YEAR(start_dt)
)

# Step 2: Group by the new patient year and count the number of new patients for each year.
yearly_new_patient_counts = first_treatment_years.PARTITION(
    name="yearly_groups", by=new_patient_year
).CALCULATE(
    year=new_patient_year,
    num_new_patients=COUNT(Treatments)
).ORDER_BY(year.ASC())

# Step 3: Calculate the year-over-year increase (NPINPI).
# PREV() gets the number of new patients from the previous year.
# A default of 0 is used for the first year in the dataset, where there is no previous year.
result = yearly_new_patient_counts.CALCULATE(
    year=year,
    num_new_patients=num_new_patients,
    NPINPI=num_new_patients - PREV(num_new_patients, by=year.ASC(), default=0)
)

[DEBUG] [Q77] Code generated by claude:
# First, let's get treatments with their start year/month and count matching adverse events
treatments_with_matching_ae = Treatments.CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    start_year=YEAR(start_dt),
    start_month=MONTH(start_dt),
    matching_ae_count=COUNT(adverse_events.WHERE(
        (YEAR(reported_dt) == YEAR(start_dt)) & 
        (MONTH(reported_dt) == MONTH(start_dt))
    ))
)

# Now group by drug and sum up all matching adverse events
drug_ae_counts = treatments_with_matching_ae.PARTITION(
    name="drug_groups", 
    by=drug_id
).CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    total_matching_ae=SUM(treatments_with_matching_ae.matching_ae_count)
)

# Join with drug information and find the top drug
result = drug_ae_counts.CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    drug_name=Drugs.WHERE(drug_id == drug_id).SINGULAR().drug_name,
    num_adverse_events=total_matching_ae
).TOP_K(1, by=num_adverse_events.DESC())

    adverse_event_count=SUM(
        treatments_used_in.CALCULATE(
            event_count=COUNT(
                adverse_events.WHERE(
                    (YEAR(reported_dt) == YEAR(start_dt)) & (MONTH(reported_dt) == MONTH(start_dt))
                )
            )
        ).event_count
    )

        (MONTH(reported_dt) == MONTH(start_dt))
    ))
)

# Now group by drug and sum up all matching adverse events
drug_ae_counts = treatments_with_matching_ae.PARTITION(
    name="drug_groups", 
    by=drug_id
).CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    total_matching_ae=SUM(treatments_with_matching_ae.matching_ae_count)
)

# Join with drug information and find the top drug
result = drug_ae_counts.CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    drug_name=Drugs.WHERE(drug_id == drug_id).SINGULAR().drug_name,
    num_adverse_events=total_matching_ae
).TOP_K(1, by=num_adverse_events.DESC())

        )

[DEBUG] [Q86] Code generated by claude:
# Filter treatments started in the past 6 months and get specialty info
recent_treatments = Treatments.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME("now")) <= 6
).CALCULATE(
    doctor_specialty=doctor.speciality,
    drug_amount=tot_drug_amt
)

# Group by specialty and calculate metrics
specialty_stats = recent_treatments.PARTITION(
    name="specialties", 
    by=doctor_specialty
).CALCULATE(
    specialty=doctor_specialty,
    num_treatments=COUNT(Treatments),
    total_drug_amount=SUM(Treatments.tot_drug_amt)
)

# Get top 3 specialties by total drug amount
result = specialty_stats.TOP_K(3, by=total_drug_amount.DESC())

).CALCULATE(

).PARTITION(
    name="specialty_groups", by=doc_specialty
).CALCULATE(

[DEBUG] [Q86] Code generated by gemini:
# First, filter treatments to include only those started in the last 6 months
# and calculate the doctor's specialty, which will be used for grouping.
recent_treatments_with_specialty = DermTreatment.Treatments.WHERE(
    start_dt >= DATETIME('now', '-6 months')
).CALCULATE(
    doc_specialty=doctor.speciality
)

# Next, partition the filtered treatments by the doctor's specialty.
# For each specialty, calculate the total number of treatments and the sum of the total drug amount.
# Finally, select the top 3 specialties based on the total drug amount in descending order.
top_specialties_by_drug_amount = recent_treatments_with_specialty.PARTITION(
    name="specialty_groups", by=(doc_specialty)
).CALCULATE(
    specialty=doc_specialty,
    number_of_treatments=COUNT(Treatments),
    total_drug_amount=SUM(Treatments.tot_drug_amt)
).TOP_K(3, by=total_drug_amount.DESC())

# Calculate the year that was 2 years ago
target_year = YEAR(DATETIME('now')) - 2

# Find doctors registered 2 years ago and get their first treatment
result = Doctors.WHERE(
    year_reg == target_year
).CALCULATE(
    last_name = last_name,
    year_of_registration = year_reg,
    first_treatment_date = prescribed_treatments.WHERE(

merchant_cpur_rate = Merchants.CALCULATE(
    merchant_name=name,
    distinct_coupons_used=NDISTINCT(transactions_received.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'successful').coupon_id),
    distinct_transactions=NDISTINCT(transactions_received.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'successful').txid)
).CALCULATE(
    merchant_name=merchant_name,
    CPUR=IFF(distinct_transactions > 0, distinct_coupons_used / distinct_transactions, 0.0)
)

# Get notifications from US and Canada users for the last 3 weeks excluding current week
us_canada_notifications = Users.WHERE(
    (LOWER(country) == LOWER('US')) | (LOWER(country) == LOWER('Canada'))
).notifications.WHERE(
    # Filter for last 3 weeks excluding current week
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", created_at, DATETIME('now')) >= 1) & 
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", created_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 3)
).CALCULATE(
    # Truncate created_at to start of week
    week_start = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    # Check if notification was sent on weekend (Saturday=7, Sunday=1)
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(created_at), (1, 7))
)

# Partition by week and calculate totals
result = us_canada_notifications.PARTITION(
    name="weekly_groups", 
    by=week_start
).CALCULATE(
    week_start = week_start,
    total_notifications = COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications = SUM(Notifications.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week_start.DESC())
[DEBUG] [Q95] Executing code for claude
[DEBUG] [Q95] DataFrame from claude is None
[DEBUG] [Q95] Code generated by gemini:
weekly_notification_summary = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    start_date_limit=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-3 weeks'),
    end_date_limit=DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
).Users.WHERE(
    ISIN(country, ('US', 'Canada'))
).notifications.WHERE(
    (created_at >= start_date_limit) & (created_at < end_date_limit)
).CALCULATE(
    notification_week=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(created_at), ('Saturday', 'Sunday'))
).PARTITION(
    name='weekly_notifications', by=(notification_week)
).CALCULATE(
    week=notification_week,
    total_notifications=COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications=SUM(Notifications.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week.ASC())

us_canada_notifications = Users.WHERE(
    (LOWER(country) == LOWER('US')) | (LOWER(country) == LOWER('Canada'))
).notifications.WHERE(
    # Filter for last 3 weeks excluding current week

).CALCULATE(

)

    by=week_start

[DEBUG] [Q95] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the time boundaries for the analysis: the start of the current week and the start of the 3-week period prior.
time_boundaries = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    start_of_current_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week'),
    start_of_period=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-3 weeks')
)

# Filter notifications to include only those from users in the US or Canada and within the specified time frame.
# Also, calculate the start of the week for each notification and a flag indicating if it was sent on a weekend.
filtered_notifications = time_boundaries.Users.WHERE(
    ISIN(country, ('US', 'Canada'))
).notifications.WHERE(
    (created_at >= start_of_period) & (created_at < start_of_current_week)
).CALCULATE(
    notification_week=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(created_at), ('Saturday', 'Sunday'))
)

# Group the filtered notifications by the week they were sent.
# For each week, count the total number of notifications and the number of weekend notifications.
weekly_notification_summary = filtered_notifications.PARTITION(
    name="weekly_groups", by=(notification_week)
).CALCULATE(
    week=notification_week,
    total_notifications=COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications=SUM(is_weekend)
)

# Assign the final result to the output variable.
result = weekly_notification_summary

# Define the time boundaries for the last 3 weeks, excluding the current week.
# This makes the start and end dates available for the subsequent query.
ewallet_context = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    start_of_current_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week'),
    start_of_period=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-3 weeks')
)

# Filter notifications to include only those sent within the defined time frame
# to users in the US or Canada. Then, for each notification, determine its
# corresponding week and whether it was sent on a weekend.
filtered_notifications = ewallet_context.Notifications.WHERE(
    (created_at >= start_of_period) &
    (created_at < start_of_current_week) &
    ISIN(user.country, ('US', 'Canada'))
).CALCULATE(
    notification_week=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(created_at), ('Saturday', 'Sunday'))
)

# Partition the filtered notifications by week and calculate the total and
# weekend notification counts for each week.
weekly_notification_summary = filtered_notifications.PARTITION(
    name='weekly_groups', by=(notification_week)
).CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=notification_week,
    total_notifications=COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications=SUM(is_weekend)
)

# First, we filter the 'Transactions' collection to isolate the relevant transactions.
# These are transactions where the sender is from the 'US', the transaction type is 'WALLET',
# and the transaction occurred within the last 7 days (inclusive of today).
recent_us_wallet_transactions = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(sending_user.country) == 'us') &
    (LOWER(transaction_type) == 'wallet') &
    (DATEDIFF("days", created_at, DATETIME('now')) < 7)
)

# Then, we use the filtered collection to calculate the final aggregate metrics.
# We count the number of transactions and sum their amounts.
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    number_of_transactions=COUNT(recent_us_wallet_transactions),
    total_transaction_amount=SUM(recent_us_wallet_transactions.amount)

# Filter balances updated in the past 7 days (inclusive)
recent_balances = UserBalances.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", updated_at, DATETIME("now")) <= 7
)

# Group by user and calculate average balance per user
user_avg_balances = recent_balances.PARTITION(
    name="user_groups", 
    by=(user_id,)
).CALCULATE(
    user_id=user_id,
    avg_balance=AVG(UserBalances.balance)
)

# Calculate the overall average of user averages
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    average_balance=AVG(user_avg_balances.avg_balance)
)

# First, filter the UserBalances to include only records from the past 7 days.
recent_balances = Ewallet.UserBalances.WHERE(DATEDIFF("days", updated_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 7)

# Next, partition the filtered balances by user to calculate the average balance for each user (AMB).
user_ambs = recent_balances.PARTITION(name="user_groups", by=(user_id)).CALCULATE(
    amb_per_user=AVG(UserBalances.balance)
)

# Finally, calculate the overall average of these individual user AMBs.
average_amb_result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    average_balance=AVG(user_ambs.amb_per_user)
)

# Calculate the date range for last 2 months excluding current month
# Start of 2 months ago
start_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-2 months')
# Start of current month (exclusive end)
end_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

# Filter transactions and calculate month
filtered_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (sender_type == 0) & 
    (created_at >= start_date) & 
    (created_at < end_date)
).CALCULATE(
    month = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month'),
    sender_id = sender_id
)

# Partition by month and count distinct senders
result = filtered_transactions.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups", 
    by=month
).CALCULATE(
    month = month,
    pmdau = NDISTINCT(Transactions.sender_id)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Define the time boundaries for the last two full months, excluding the current one.
start_of_current_month = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
start_of_period = DATETIME(start_of_current_month, '-2 months')

# Filter transactions to the relevant time period and sender type,
# and create a column representing the month of the transaction.
transactions_in_period = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (created_at >= start_of_period) &
    (created_at < start_of_current_month) &
    (sender_type == 0)
).CALCULATE(
    transaction_month=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month')
)

# Partition by the transaction month and calculate the number of distinct senders for each month.
# This follows the provided definition of PMDAU.
pmdau_by_month = transactions_in_period.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups", by=(transaction_month)
).CALCULATE(
    month=transaction_month,
    pmdau=NDISTINCT(Transactions.sender_id)
)

# Define the time boundaries for the last two full months.
start_of_current_month = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
start_of_period = DATETIME(start_of_current_month, '-2 months')

# Filter transactions to the specified time window and sender type, and prepare date fields for grouping.
transactions_in_range = Transactions.WHERE(
    (created_at >= start_of_period) &
    (created_at < start_of_current_month) &
    (sender_type == 0)
).CALCULATE(
    tx_month_start=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month'),
    tx_day_start=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of day')
)

# First, partition by day to calculate the Daily Active Users (DAU).
daily_active_users = transactions_in_range.PARTITION(
    name="daily_groups", 
    by=(tx_month_start, tx_day_start)
).CALCULATE(
    tx_month_start,
    dau=NDISTINCT(Transactions.sender_id)
)

# Second, partition the daily results by month to calculate the average DAU, which is the PMDAU.
pmdau_by_month = daily_active_users.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups", 
    by=(tx_month_start)
).CALCULATE(
    month=tx_month_start,
    pmdau=AVG(daily_groups.dau)
)

# Assign the final result, ordered by month.
result = pmdau_by_month.ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Get transactions from the previous month
prev_month_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (created_at >= DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-1 month')) &
    (created_at < DATETIME('now', 'start of month'))
)

# Calculate STR
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    total_transactions=COUNT(prev_month_transactions),
    successful_transactions=COUNT(prev_month_transactions.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'success')),
    str_percentage=100.0 * COUNT(prev_month_transactions.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'success')) / COUNT(prev_month_transactions)
)

# Define the date boundaries for the previous month using contextless expressions
start_of_previous_month = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-1 month')
start_of_current_month = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

# Create a collection of transactions that occurred in the previous month
transactions_in_previous_month = Transactions.WHERE(
    (created_at >= start_of_previous_month) & (created_at < start_of_current_month)
)

# Calculate the total and successful transaction counts, then compute the STR
previous_month_str = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    successful_tx_count=COUNT(transactions_in_previous_month.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'success')),
    total_tx_count=COUNT(transactions_in_previous_month)
).CALCULATE(
    str=IFF(total_tx_count > 0, 100.0 * successful_tx_count / total_tx_count, 0.0)
)

start_of_previous_month = DATETIME(start_of_current_month, '-1 month')

# Filter transactions to include only those from the previous month
transactions_in_last_month = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (created_at >= start_of_previous_month) & (created_at < start_of_current_month)
)

# Calculate the number of successful and total transactions
transaction_counts = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    successful_transactions=COUNT(transactions_in_last_month.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'success')),
    total_transactions=COUNT(transactions_in_last_month)
)

wallet_str_previous_month = transaction_counts.CALCULATE(
    str=IFF(total_transactions > 0, successful_transactions / total_transactions, 0.0)
)

# Step 1: Filter transactions to include only those made in 2023 by active users, and which were successful.
# Also, create a 'transaction_month' field to prepare for grouping.
transactions_in_2023 = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (YEAR(created_at) == 2023) &
    (LOWER(status) == 'completed') &
    (HAS(sending_user) == 1) &
    (LOWER(sending_user.status) == 'active')
).CALCULATE(
    transaction_month=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month')
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered transactions by month.
monthly_transactions = transactions_in_2023.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups",
    by=(transaction_month)
)

# Step 3: For each month, calculate the number of distinct sending users and select the month.
# Then, sort the results by date in ascending order.
active_users_per_month = monthly_transactions.CALCULATE(
    month=transaction_month,
    num_of_active_users=NDISTINCT(Transactions.sending_user.uid)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# First, filter active users and their successful transactions in 2023
active_users_transactions_2023 = Users.WHERE(
    LOWER(status) == LOWER('active')
).transactions_sent.WHERE(
    (YEAR(created_at) == 2023) & 
    (LOWER(status) == LOWER('successful')) &
    (sender_type == 1)  # Assuming 1 is for users, 0 for merchants based on the int32 type
).CALCULATE(
    user_id=sender_id,
    month_date=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month')
)

# Partition by month and count distinct users
monthly_active_users = active_users_transactions_2023.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=month_date
).CALCULATE(
    month=month_date,
    active_users=NDISTINCT(active_users_transactions_2023.user_id)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

result = monthly_active_users

INFO:google_genai.models:AFC is enabled with max remote calls: 10.
INFO:httpx:HTTP Request: POST https://us-east5-aiplatform.googleapis.com/v1/projects/solid-drive-448717-p8/locations/us-east5/publishers/anthropic/models/claude-opus-4@20250514:streamRawPredict "HTTP/1.1 200 OK"
[DEBUG] [Q118] Code generated by claude:
# First, let's get users with their country and filter for those who sent transactions with sender_type = 0
users_with_transactions = Users.WHERE(
    HAS(transactions_sent.WHERE(sender_type == 0)) == 1
).CALCULATE(
    user_country = country,
    user_id = uid,
    total_sent = SUM(transactions_sent.WHERE(sender_type == 0).amount)
)

# Now partition by country to aggregate the data
country_stats = users_with_transactions.PARTITION(
    name="countries", 
    by=user_country
).CALCULATE(
    country = user_country,
    num_distinct_users = COUNT(Users),
    total_transaction_amount = SUM(Users.total_sent)
)

# Get the top 5 countries by total transaction amount
result = country_stats.TOP_K(5, by=total_transaction_amount.DESC())

top_5_countries_by_sent_amount = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    sender_type == 0
).CALCULATE(
    sender_country=sending_user.country,
    tx_amount=amount,
    sender_uid=sending_user.uid
).PARTITION(
    name="countries", by=(sender_country)
).CALCULATE(
    country=sender_country,
    total_transaction_amount=SUM(Transactions.tx_amount),
    num_distinct_users=NDISTINCT(Transactions.sender_uid)
).TOP_K(5, by=total_transaction_amount.DESC())

).CALCULATE(
    user_country=country,
    user_id=uid,
    total_sent=SUM(transactions_sent.WHERE(sender_type == 0).amount)

    name="countries", 
    by=user_country
).CALCULATE(

    num_distinct_users=COUNT(Users),
    total_transaction_amount=SUM(Users.total_sent)
)
